\f0\b \cf0 Caveats
\f1\b0 \
The audit can assess Acting branch maturity at research, synthesis, audit, revision, final audit and output-specificity level. It cannot verify true Acting-only rendered labels, comparison-page behaviour, timestamp export parity or Acting-only report quality because no Acting-only live outputs exist yet. The MT acting+song reports are useful bridge evidence only; they are not Acting release sign-off.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 12. Acting Patch Decision\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\fs24 \cf0 Decision: No Acting patch required; branch is mature enough for implementation planning with limitations.
\f1\b0 \
Rationale:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls3\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Acting has completed all research/rubric stages through Output Specificity / Non-Regression Test Mapping.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Source coverage is broad and statused: 43 rows across B1\'96B4, with inaccessible, partial, process-only and course-shaped limitations explicitly carried.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17, ACTING-AUDIT-I01\'96I15, ACTING-AUDIT-R01\'96R16, ACTING-REV-C01\'96C16, ACTING-REV-S01\'96S15 and ACTING-OS-R/T IDs form a complete traceability chain.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The final audit already concluded there were no critical blockers requiring a return to ACTING-REV.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 The remaining weaknesses are known limitations rather than evidence failures: live-output absence, label/render verification, comedy thinness, physical/fight source shape, methodology-neutral descriptor range and broad neurodivergence operational detail.\
\ls3\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Under lean v5, incomplete live-output QA is not a reason to repeat research automatically; it is a later QA and release-sign-off gate.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Critical blockers: none.\
Patch scope if needed: not applicable.\
What should not be reopened:\
\pard\tx220\tx720\pardeftab720\li720\fi-720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\ls4\ilvl0\cf0 \kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Full Acting research.\
\ls4\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 ACTING-SYN, ACTING-AUDIT, ACTING-REV or ACTING-FINAL-AUDIT.\
\ls4\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Source extraction, except narrow provenance verification if future implementation planning specifically needs it.\
\ls4\ilvl0\kerning1\expnd0\expndtw0 \outl0\strokewidth0 {\listtext	\uc0\u8226 	}\expnd0\expndtw0\kerning0
\outl0\strokewidth0 \strokec2 Comedy, physical/fight or neurodivergence research unless the product later needs a first-class subtype or access-specific branch.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0
\cf0 Deep Research required: no.\
Standard ChatGPT sufficient: yes, for future implementation-readiness consolidation and live QA planning.\
Live outputs required for patch: no patch is required. Live outputs are required later for release sign-off.\
Can proceed before implementation planning: yes. Acting may move into cross-branch implementation planning with explicit limitations.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0

\f0\b\fs36 \cf0 14. Reusable Handoff Pack\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa240\partightenfactor0

\f1\b0\fs24 \cf0 ACTING-V5-MATURITY-AUDIT concludes that the Acting branch is mature enough for implementation planning with explicit limitations and does not require an ACTING-PATCH. Acting has completed the full research/rubric pathway through ACTING-OUTPUT-SPEC: ACTING-S0, ACTING-B1\'96B4, ACTING-SYN, ACTING-AUDIT, ACTING-REV, ACTING-FINAL-AUDIT and ACTING-OUTPUT-SPEC are all present and traceable. The branch preserves the shared TapeCoach six-field model, acting_scene / monologue weights, server-side recomputation, caps, blockers, verdict thresholds, role-fit bounds, Step 1 / Step 2 pipeline, report schema, safety/material/accessibility scrubs and the MT acting + song anchor.\
The strongest Acting assets are form specificity, spoken voice label containment, screen/self-tape task evidence, monologue/text-type handling, reader-as-process support, self-tape assessability and anti-polish rules, speech audibility versus speech performance, no-brief role/world restraint, live-room-only claim boundaries, accessibility-safe non-deficit handling, methodology-neutrality, external-framework weighting caution, generic-feedback suppression and timestamp/next-take specificity. Final audit approved the revised Acting baseline with explicit limitations and no return-to-revision blocker.\
Remaining weaknesses are limitation items, not patch triggers: no true Acting-only live outputs, unverified frontend labels, comparison-page labels, renderer/export timestamp behaviour, structural vocal/speech proxy semantics, thin comedy evidence, specialist-course-shaped physical/fight evidence, limited methodology-neutral descriptor range, broad neurodivergence operational detail and unresolved Royal Central source limitation. True Acting live-output QA remains required before product release sign-off, especially for spoken-only label containment, monologue/no-brief outputs, reader-mediated scenes, screen task eyeline handling, access-sensitive outputs, timestamp density and generic-feedback enforcement.\
Traceability basis: ACTING-S001\'96S043; ACTING-B1-F01\'96F10, B2-F01\'96F09, B3-F01\'96F08, B4-F01\'96F10; ACTING-SYN-F01\'96F17; ACTING-AUDIT-I01\'96I15 and R01\'96R16; ACTING-REV-C01\'96C16 and S01\'96S15; ACTING-FINAL-D01\'96D02; ACTING-OS-R01\'96R19, T01\'96T20 and I01\'96I10.\
\pard\pardeftab720\sa298\partightenfactor0
